## Entry 4e371483
**id**: 4e371483

**Role**: toolResult

{
  description = "Minimum development shell for Rust, libcurl, and libsqlite3";

  inputs = {
    nixpkgs.url = "github:nixos/nixpkgs/nixos-unstable";
  };

  outputs = { self, nixpkgs }:
    let
      system = "aarch64-linux";
      pkgs = import nixpkgs {
        inherit system;
        config.allowUnfree = true;
      };
      shell = pkgs.mkShell {
        buildInputs = [
          pkgs.rustc
          pkgs.cargo
          pkgs.curl
          pkgs.sqlite
          pkgs.fossil
          pkgs.pkg-config
          pkgs.openssl
        ];
        shellHook = ''
          export CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/cargo-target
        '';
      };
    in
    {
      devShells.default = shell;
      devShells.aarch64-linux.default = shell;
    };
}

## Entry 2ca425bb
**id**: 2ca425bb

**Role**: toolResult

use rusqlite::{Connection};
use curl;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

#[test]
fn test_versions() {
    let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().expect("Failed to open in-memory database");
    let sqlite_version: String = conn.query_row("SELECT sqlite_version()", [], |row| row.get(0))
        .expect("Failed to get sqlite version");
    println!("SQLite version: {}", sqlite_version);
    println!("curl is available");
    println!("openssl is available");
}

#[test]
fn test_curl_request() {
    let mut handle = curl::easy::Easy::new();
    handle.url("https://ontouchstart.github.io").expect("Failed to set URL");

    let response_data = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
    let response_data_clone = Arc::clone(&response_data);
    handle.write_function(move |data| {
        let mut buffer = response_data_clone.lock().unwrap();
        buffer.extend_from_slice(data);
        Ok(data.len())
    }).expect("Failed to set write function");

    handle.perform().expect("Failed to perform request");

    let response_code = handle.response_code().expect("Failed to get response code");
    let body = response_data.lock().unwrap();
    println!("Response code: {}", response_code);
    println!("Response body: {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&body));

    assert_eq!(response_code, 200);
}

fn main() {
    println!("Hello, world!");
}

## Entry b83a1e78
**id**: b83a1e78

**Role**: toolResult

use rusqlite::{params, Connection, Result};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_open_in_memory_connection() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory();
        assert!(conn.is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_create_table_and_insert() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name  TEXT NOT NULL,
                age   INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Alice", 30],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Bob", 25],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, age FROM users ORDER BY age").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, i32>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Bob".to_string(), 25));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Alice".to_string(), 30));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_prepared_statement() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE products (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("INSERT INTO products (name) VALUES (?)").unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Laptop"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Phone"]).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name FROM products").unwrap();
        let names: Vec<String> = stmt.query_map([], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap().map(|r| r.unwrap()).collect();
        assert_eq!(names, vec!["Laptop", "Phone"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance + 100 WHERE id = 2", []).unwrap(); // This will fail if account 2 doesn't exist
        // Let's fix this to be a valid transaction
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        tx.commit().unwrap();

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 900);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction_rollback() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        // Drop transaction without commit to rollback
        drop(tx);

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 1000);
    }
}

fn main() {
    println!("Run 'cargo test' to see the rusqlite tests.");
}

## Entry b242a33a
**id**: b242a33a

**Role**: assistant

> **Thinking**: 
> The user wants to know how to find accurate and up-to-date documentation for `rusqlite` without an internet connection.

Since I'm in a development environment with `cargo` and `nix`, there are several ways to do this:

1.  **Cargo Docs**: Cargo can generate documentation for all dependencies.
2.  **Rustdoc in the `target` directory**: If the project was built, `cargo doc` generates HTML files in `target/doc`.
3.  **Library Source Code**: One can read the source code directly (though not documentation, it's the "ground truth").
4.  **Nix Store**: Since I'm using Nix, I could potentially find where the library is installed, but `rusqlite` is a Rust crate, so it's usually in the cargo home.
5.  **Local documentation from crates.io**: If the user has downloaded the crates, the docs are often available.

The most "standard" and "accurate" way for a Rust developer is `cargo doc`.

I should explain `cargo doc`.

Wait, I should check if I can run `cargo doc` here to see if it works.

To find accurate and up-to-date documentation for the specific version of a library (like `rusqlite`) you are using without an internet connection, the standard Rust approach is to use **`cargo doc`**.

### 1. Using `cargo doc`
Cargo can generate HTML documentation for your project and all of its dependencies (including `rusqlite`) locally. This documentation is generated directly from the source code of the crates you have downloaded.

To generate the documentation, run:
```bash
nix develop --command cargo doc --open
```
*(Note: If you are offline, the `--open` flag might fail if it tries to open a browser, but the HTML files will still be generated.)*

**Where to find the files:**
The documentation will be generated in your project's `target/doc` directory. You can navigate there and open the `rusqlite` folder in any web browser to see the full API reference.

### 2. Why this is the best method:
*   **Accuracy:** It uses the exact source code of the version specified in your `Cargo.toml`.
*   **Completeness:** It includes all public modules, structs, traits, and functions.
*   **Offline Capability:** Once the crates are downloaded (which you have already done by running `cargo add` or `cargo build`), `cargo doc` requires no internet connection.

### 3. Alternative: Checking the source code
If you need to see the internal implementation details or exactly how a function is defined:
1.  Look at your `Cargo.lock` file to find the exact version.
2.  Use `cargo metadata` to find the local path to the `rusqlite` crate.
3.  Navigate to that path to read the `.rs` files directly.

### Summary Command for your environment:
To view the docs now that you have the project set up:
```bash
nix develop --command cargo doc --no-deps --omit <path_to_project>
# Or for everything including rusqlite:
nix develop --command cargo doc --all-features
```
*(Since you are in a Nix shell, you may need to ensure your browser is accessible to view the resulting HTML files in `target/doc`.)*

### 1. Generate Local Documentation (Recommended)
Since you have the source code downloaded, you can use Cargo to generate HTML documentation for your project and all its dependencies (including `rusqlite`). This is the most accurate way to see the API.

Run this command:
```bash
nix develop --command cargo doc --no-deps --omit <path_to_your_project>
```
Actually, to include `rusqlite`, run:

Since you asked specifically to find the **cached source code**, Cargo stores the source of all downloaded crates in a central registry folder.

## Entry fdbb742b
**id**: fdbb742b

**Role**: toolResult

{
  description = "Minimum development shell for Rust, libcurl, and libsqlite3";

  inputs = {
    nixpkgs.url = "github:nixos/nixpkgs/nixos-unstable";
  };

  outputs = { self, nixpkgs }:
    let
      system = "aarch64-linux";
      pkgs = import nixpkgs {
        inherit system;
        config.allowUnfree = true;
      };
      shell = pkgs.mkShell {
        buildInputs = [
          pkgs.rustc
          pkgs.cargo
          pkgs.curl
          pkgs.sqlite
          pkgs.fossil
          pkgs.pkg-config
          pkgs.openssl
        ];
        shellHook = ''
          export CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/cargo-target
        '';
      };
    in
    {
      devShells.default = shell;
      devShells.aarch64-linux.default = shell;
    };
}

## Entry 40dca66e
**id**: 40dca66e

**Role**: toolResult

//! Rusqlite is an ergonomic wrapper for using SQLite from Rust.
//!
//! Historically, the API was based on the one from
//! [`rust-postgres`](https://github.com/sfackler/rust-postgres). However, the
//! two have diverged in many ways, and no compatibility between the two is
//! intended.
//!
//! ```rust
//! use rusqlite::{params, Connection, Result};
//!
//! #[derive(Debug)]
//! struct Person {
//!     id: i32,
//!     name: String,
//!     data: Option<Vec<u8>>,
//! }
//!
//! fn main() -> Result<()> {
//!     let conn = Connection::open_in_memory()?;
//!
//!     conn.execute(
//!         "CREATE TABLE person (
//!             id   INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
//!             name TEXT NOT NULL,
//!             data BLOB
//!         )",
//!         (), // empty list of parameters.
//!     )?;
//!     let me = Person {
//!         id: 0,
//!         name: "Steven".to_string(),
//!         data: None,
//!     };
//!     conn.execute(
//!         "INSERT INTO person (name, data) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
//!         (&me.name, &me.data),
//!     )?;
//!
//!     let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT id, name, data FROM person")?;
//!     let person_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
//!         Ok(Person {
//!             id: row.get(0)?,
//!             name: row.get(1)?,
//!             data: row.get(2)?,
//!         })
//!     })?;
//!
//!     for person in person_iter {
//!         println!("Found person {:?}", person?);
//!     }
//!     Ok(())
//! }
//! ```
#![warn(missing_docs)]
#![cfg_attr(docsrs, feature(doc_cfg))]

pub use fallible_iterator;
pub use fallible_streaming_iterator;

#[cfg(not(all(
    target_family = "wasm",
    target_os = "unknown",
    any(test, feature = "ffi-sqlite-wasm-rs")
)))]
pub use libsqlite3_sys as ffi;
#[cfg(all(
    target_family = "wasm",
    target_os = "unknown",
    any(test, feature = "ffi-sqlite-wasm-rs")
))]
pub use sqlite_wasm_rs as ffi;

use std::cell::RefCell;
use std::default::Default;
use std::ffi::{c_char, c_int, c_uint, CStr, CString};
use std::fmt;

use std::path::Path;
use std::result;
use std::str;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

#[cfg(feature = "cache")]
use crate::cache::StatementCache;
use crate::inner_connection::InnerConnection;
use crate::raw_statement::RawStatement;
use crate::types::ValueRef;

pub use crate::bind::BindIndex;
#[cfg(feature = "cache")]
pub use crate::cache::CachedStatement;
#[cfg(feature = "column_decltype")]
pub use crate::column::Column;
#[cfg(feature = "column_metadata")]
pub use crate::column::ColumnMetadata;
pub use crate::error::{to_sqlite_error, Error};
pub use crate::ffi::ErrorCode;
#[cfg(feature = "load_extension")]
pub use crate::load_extension_guard::LoadExtensionGuard;
pub use crate::params::{params_from_iter, Params, ParamsFromIter};

## Entry bfd82b54
**id**: bfd82b54

**Role**: assistant

> **Thinking**: 
> Okay, I have a good overview.

### What I learned from `lib.rs`:
1.  **Core Components**:
    *   `Connection`: The main entry point. `Connection::open_in_memory()` is the standard way to get a temporary DB.
    *   `Statement`: Used for executing SQL. Prepared statements are encouraged via `conn.prepare()`.
    *   `params!`: A macro for easily passing parameters to queries.
    *   `query_map`: A convenient way to map rows to custom Rust structs.
    *   `Row`: Used to access columns by index or name.
    *   `Transaction`: Supports atomic operations.
2.  **Error Handling**:
    *   `Error` type handles various SQLite errors.
    *   `Result` type is used throughout.
3.  **Features**:
    *   `cache`: Supports statement caching.
    *   `ffi-sqlite-wasm-rs`: Support for WebAssembly.
    *   `column_decltype` and `column_metadata`: Provide more info about columns.
    *   `load_extension`: Allows loading SQLite extensions.
4.  **Types**:
    *   `ValueRef`: For referring to values in a row.
    *   `BindIndex`: For binding parameters.

### Plan for more tests:
Based on the features and modules I saw, here is a plan to expand the tests:

1.  **Data Types & Conversions**:
    *   Test inserting and retrieving different types: `i32`, `i64`, `f64`, `String`, `Vec<u8>` (BLOB).
    *   Test `Option<T>` values (NULLs in SQLite).
2.  **Advanced Querying**:
    *   Test `query_row` for single results.
    *   Test complex `WHERE` clauses and `ORDER BY`.
    *   Test `LIMIT` and `OFFSET`.
3.  **Transaction Isolation**:
    *   Test nested transactions (if supported) or multiple sequential transactions.
    *   Test explicit `ROLLBACK` and ensuring state is unchanged.
4.  **Prepared Statements & Caching** (if `cache` feature is enabled):
    *   Test reusing a `Statement` multiple times with different parameters.
    *   Test the `StatementCache` behavior.
5.  **Complex SQL**:
    *   Test `JOIN` operations.
    *   Test `GROUP BY` and aggregate functions (`SUM`, `COUNT`, `AVG`).
    *   Test `CREATE INDEX` and its effect on performance/query results.
6.  **Error Handling**:
    *   Test intentional errors: inserting a string into an integer column, primary key violations, and table-not-found errors.
    *   Verify that `Error` provides useful information.
7.  **File-based Database**:
    *   Test creating a persistent file-based database (not just in-memory).
    *   Test concurrent access or multiple connections to the same file (if applicable).

I will start by implementing the **Data Types & Conversions** and **Advanced Querying** tests.


I have examined the `rusqlite` source code in the cached registry. Here is a summary of what I've learned and a plan for expanding our test suite.

### Summary of Findings (RTFM)

1.  **Core API Structure**:
    *   **`Connection`**: The primary object for interacting with the database. `Connection::open_in_memory()` is the standard for testing.
    *   **`Statement` & `prepare()`**: Prepared statements are the recommended way to execute queries. They provide better performance and security against SQL injection.
    *   **`params!` macro**: A crucial tool for cleanly binding Rust variables to SQL placeholders (e.g., `?1`, `?2`).
    *   **`query_map()`**: The standard ergonomic way to transform SQL result rows into high-level Rust structs/enums.
    *   **`Row` object**: Provides methods like `get(index)` or `get(name)` to extract typed data from a result set.

2.  **Key Features and Modules**:
    *   **Caching**: The `cache` feature enables statement caching, which is important for performance in high-frequency queries.
    *   **Error Handling**: The `Error` type provides structured information about SQLite-specific failures (like constraint violations or syntax errors).
    *   **Wasm Support**: There is dedicated support for `sqlite-wasm-rs` for web targets.
    *   **Type Mapping**: `ValueRef` and `BindIndex` are internal types that handle the conversion between Rust types and SQLite's C-types.

3.  **Safety and Ergonomics**:
    *   The library heavily uses Rust's ownership model to ensure that connections and statements are closed properly when they go out of scope.
    *   It provides "fallible" iterators to handle potential database errors during row iteration.

---

### Testing Plan

I will expand the test suite in `rusqlite_test` following these priorities:

#### Phase 1: Data Integrity & Type System (Next Step)
*   **Multiple Types**: Test inserting and retrieving `i64`, `f64`, `String`, and `Vec<u8>` (BLOBs).
*   **Nullability**: Test `Option<T>` mapping to SQLite `NULL` values.
*   **Complex Structs**: Create a more complex struct (e.g., `User` with a nested `Profile` struct) and test its full lifecycle.

#### Phase 2: Advanced Querying
*   **Aggregates**: Test `COUNT`, `SUM`, and `AVG` functions.
*   **Joins**: Test a simple `INNER JOIN` between two tables (e.g., `Users` and `Posts`).
*   **Limits/Offsets**: Verify that `LIMIT` and `OFFSET` correctly paginate results.

#### Phase 3: Transactional Integrity & Robustness
*   **Rollback Scenarios**: Explicitly test that a failed transaction (e.g., a primary key violation) correctly rolls back all preceding changes in that block.
*   **Error Verification**: Purposefully trigger errors (e.g., "Table Not Found", "Constraint Violation") and assert that the returned `rusqlite::Error` matches the expected code.

#### Phase 4: Performance & Features (Optional/Advanced)
*   **Statement Reuse**: Test executing the same prepared statement multiple times with different parameters to ensure no state leakage.
*   **File Persistence**: Test creating and reading from a persistent `.db` file on disk.

**Next action**: I will begin implementing the **Data Integrity & Type System** tests.

Original `test_create_table_and_insert`:
```rust
    #[test]
    fn test_create_table_and_insert() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name  TEXT NOT NULL,
                age   INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Alice", 30],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Bob", 25],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, age FROM users ORDER BY age").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, i32>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Bob".to_string(), 25));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Alice".to_string(), 30));
    }
```

## Entry 2bd31733

**Role**: assistant

> **Thinking**: 
> Test 4 passed. Now I'll add the fifth test.
Original `test_transaction_rollback`:
```rust
    #[test]
    fn test_transaction_rollback() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        // Drop transaction without commit to rollback
        drop(tx);

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 1000);
    }
```

## Entry 502f4ff0
**id**: 502f4ff0

**Role**: toolResult

use rusqlite::{params, Connection, Result};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_open_in_memory_connection() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory();
        assert!(conn.is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_create_table_and_insert() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name  TEXT NOT NULL,
                age   INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Alice", 30],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Bob", 25],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, age FROM users ORDER BY age").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, i32>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Bob".to_string(), 25));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Alice".to_string(), 30));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_prepared_statement() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE products (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("INSERT INTO products (name) VALUES (?)").unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Laptop"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Phone"]).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name FROM products").unwrap();
        let names: Vec<String> = stmt.query_map([], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap().map(|r| r.unwrap()).collect();
        assert_eq!(names, vec!["Laptop", "Phone"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        tx.commit().unwrap();

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 900);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction_rollback() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        // Drop transaction without commit to rollback
        drop(tx);

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 1000);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_multiple_types_and_nullability() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE items (
                id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name        TEXT NOT NULL,
                price       REAL NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                data        BLOB
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        // Test with a NULL description
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO items (name, price, description, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
            params!["Widget", 19.99, None::<String>, Some(vec![1u8, 2, 3])],
        ).unwrap();

        // Test with a non-NULL description
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO items (name, price, description, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
            params!["Gadget", 29.99, Some("Useful tool".to_string()), None::<Vec<u8>>],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, price, description, data FROM items").unwrap();
        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((
                row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
                row.get::<_, f64>(1)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<String>>(2)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<Vec<u8>>>(3)?,
            ))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {
            results.push(item.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Widget".to_string(), 19.99, None, Some(vec![1, 2, 3])));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Gadget".to_string(), 29.99, Some("Useful tool".to_string()), None));
    }
}

fn main() {
    println!("Hello, world!");
}

    #[test]

        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE products (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)",

        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("INSERT INTO products (name) VALUES (?)").unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Laptop"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Phone"]).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(names, vec!["Laptop", "Phone"]);

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();

    #[test]
    fn test_complex_struct() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name     TEXT NOT NULL,
                bio      TEXT,
                age      INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, bio, age) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
            params!["Steven", Some("Software Engineer".to_string()), 30],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT id, name, bio, age FROM users").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok(User {
                id: row.get(0)?,
                name: row.get(1)?,
                profile: Profile {
                    bio: row.get(2)?,
                    age: row.get(3)?,
                },
            })
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(results[0], User {
            id: 1,
            name: "Steven".to_string(),
            profile: Profile {
                bio: Some("Software Engineer".to_string()),
                age: 30,
            },
        });
    }

## Entry 20767453
**id**: 20767453

**Role**: toolResult

use rusqlite::{params, Connection, Result};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    struct Profile {
        bio: Option<String>,
        age: i32,
    }

    #[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    struct User {
        id: i32,
        name: String,
        profile: Profile,
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_open_in_memory_connection() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory();
        assert!(conn.is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_create_table_and_insert() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name  TEXT NOT NULL,
                age   INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Alice", 30],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Bob", 25],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, age FROM users ORDER BY age").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, i32>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Bob".to_string(), 25));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Alice".to_string(), 30));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_prepared_statement() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE products (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("INSERT INTO products (name) VALUES (?)").unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Laptop"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Phone"]).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name FROM products").unwrap();
        let names: Vec<String> = stmt.query_map([], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap().map(|r| r.unwrap()).collect();
        assert_eq!(names, vec!["Laptop", "Phone"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        tx.commit().unwrap();

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 900);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction_rollback() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        // Drop transaction without commit to rollback
        drop(tx);

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 1000);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_multiple_types_and_nullability() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE items (
                id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name        TEXT NOT NULL,
                price       REAL NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                data        BLOB
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        // Test with a NULL description
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO items (name, price, description, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
            params!["Widget", 19.99, None::<String>, Some(vec![1u8, 2, 3])],
        ).unwrap();

        // Test with a non-NULL description
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO items (name, price, description, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
            params!["Gadget", 29.99, Some("Useful tool".to_string()), None::<Vec<u8>>],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, price, description, data FROM items").unwrap();
        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((
                row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
                row.get::<_, f64>(1)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<String>>(2)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<Vec<u8>>>(3)?,
            ))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {
            results.push(item.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Widget".to_string(), 19.99, None, Some(vec![1, 2, 3])));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Gadget".to_string(), 29.99, Some("Useful tool".to_string()), None));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_complex_struct() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name     TEXT NOT NULL,
                bio      TEXT,
                age      INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, bio, age) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
            params!["Steven", Some("Software Engineer".to_string()), 30],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT id, name, bio, age FROM users").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok(User {
                id: row.get(0)?,
                name: row.get(1)?,
                profile: Profile {
                    bio: row.get(2)?,
                    age: row.get(3)?,
                },
            })
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(results[0], User {
            id: 1,
            name: "Steven".to_string(),
            profile: Profile {
                bio: Some("Software Engineer".to_string()),
                age: 30,
            },
        });
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_aggregates() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE sales (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                amount REAL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (10.5)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (20.0)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (30.5)", []).unwrap();

        let sum: f64 = conn.query_row("SELECT SUM(amount) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        let avg: f64 = conn.query_row("SELECT AVG(amount) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        let count: i64 = conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(sum, 61.0);
        assert_eq!(avg, 20.333333333333332);
        assert_eq!(count, 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_joins_and_limits() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE authors (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE books (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                title TEXT NOT NULL,
                author_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                FOREIGN KEY(author_id) REFERENCES authors(id)
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES ('J.R.R. Tolkien')", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES ('George R.R. Martin')", []).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('The Hobbit', 1)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('The Silmarillion', 1)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('A Game of Thrones', 2)", []).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
            "SELECT b.title, a.name 
             FROM books b 
             JOIN authors a ON b.author_id = a.id 
             ORDER BY b.title 
             LIMIT 2",
        ).unwrap();

        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, String>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {
            results.push(item.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("A Game of Thrones", "George R.R. Martin"));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("The Hobbit", "J.R.R. Tolkien"));
    }
}

fn main() {
    println!("Hello, world!");
}

```rust
    #[test]
    fn test_transaction_rollback() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        // Drop transaction without commit to rollback
        drop(tx);

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 1000);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_multiple_types_and_nullability() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE items (
                id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name        TEXT NOT NULL,
                price       REAL NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                data        BLOB
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        // Test with a NULL description
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO items (name, price, description, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
            params!["Widget", 19.99, None::<String>, Some(vec![1u8, 2, 3])],
        ).unwrap();

        // Test with a non-NULL description
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO items (name, price, description, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
            params!["Gadget", 29.99, Some("Useful tool".to_string()), None::<Vec<u8>>],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, price, description, data FROM items").unwrap();
        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((
                row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
                row.get::<_, f64>(1)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<String>>(2)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<Vec<u8>>>(3)?,
            ))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {
            results.push(item.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Widget".to_string(), 19.99, None, Some(vec![1, 2, 3])));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Gadget".to_string(), 29.99, Some("Useful tool".to_string()), None));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_complex_struct() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name     TEXT NOT NULL,
                bio      TEXT,
                age      INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, bio, age) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
            params!["Steven", Some("Software Engineer".to_string()), 30],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT id, name, bio, age FROM users").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok(User {
                id: row.get(0)?,
                name: row.get(1)?,
                profile: Profile {
                    bio: row.get(2)?,
                    age: row.get(3)?,
                },
            })
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(results[0], User {
            id: 1,
            name: "Steven".to_string(),
            profile: Profile {
                bio: Some("Software Engineer".to_string()),
                age: 30,
            },
        });
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_aggregates() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE sales (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                amount REAL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (10.5)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (20.0)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (30.5)", []).unwrap();

        let sum: f64 = conn.query_row("SELECT SUM(amount) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        let avg: f64 = conn.query_row("SELECT AVG(amount) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        let count: i64 = conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(sum, 61.0);
        assert_eq!(avg, 20.333333333333332);
        assert_eq!(count, 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_joins_and_limits() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE authors (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE books (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                title TEXT NOT NULL,
                author_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                FOREIGN KEY(author_id) REFERENCES authors(id)
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES ('J.R.R. Tolkien')", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES ('George R.R. Martin')", []).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('The Hobbit', 1)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('The Silmarillion', 1)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('A Game of Thrones', 2)", []).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
            "SELECT b.title, a.name 
             FROM books b 
             JOIN authors a ON b.author_id = a.id 
             ORDER BY b.title 
             LIMIT 2",
        ).unwrap();

        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, String>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {
            results.push(item.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("A Game of Thrones", "George R.R. Martin"));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("The Hobbit", "J.R.R. Tolkien"));
    }
}
```

## Entry 53fb797f
**id**: 53fb797f

**Role**: toolResult

use rusqlite::{params, Connection, Result};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    struct Profile {
        bio: Option<String>,
        age: i32,
    }

    #[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    struct User {
        id: i32,
        name: String,
        profile: Profile,
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_open_in_memory_connection() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory();
        assert!(conn.is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_create_table_and_insert() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name  TEXT NOT NULL,
                age   INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Alice", 30],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Bob", 25],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, age FROM users ORDER BY age").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, i32>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Bob".to_string(), 25));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Alice".to_string(), 30));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_prepared_statement() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE products (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("INSERT INTO products (name) VALUES (?)").unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Laptop"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Phone"]).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name FROM products").unwrap();
        let names: Vec<String> = stmt.query_map([], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap().map(|r| r.unwrap()).collect();
        assert_eq!(names, vec!["Laptop", "Phone"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        tx.commit().unwrap();

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 900);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction_rollback() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        // Drop transaction without commit to rollback
        drop(tx);

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 1000);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_multiple_types_and_nullability() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE items (
                id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name        TEXT NOT NULL,
                price       REAL NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                data        BLOB
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        // Test with a NULL description
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO items (name, price, description, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
            params!["Widget", 19.99, None::<String>, Some(vec![1u8, 2, 3])],
        ).unwrap();

        // Test with a non-NULL description
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO items (name, price, description, data) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
            params!["Gadget", 29.99, Some("Useful tool".to_string()), None::<Vec<u8>>],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, price, description, data FROM items").unwrap();
        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((
                row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
                row.get::<_, f64>(1)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<String>>(2)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<Vec<u8>>>(3)?,
            ))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {
            results.push(item.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Widget".to_string(), 19.99, None, Some(vec![1, 2, 3])));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Gadget".to_string(), 29.99, Some("Useful tool".to_string()), None));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_complex_struct() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name     TEXT NOT NULL,
                bio      TEXT,
                age      INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, bio, age) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
            params!["Steven", Some("Software Engineer".to_string()), 30],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT id, name, bio, age FROM users").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok(User {
                id: row.get(0)?,
                name: row.get(1)?,
                profile: Profile {
                    bio: row.get(2)?,
                    age: row.get(3)?,
                },
            })
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(results[0], User {
            id: 1,
            name: "Steven".to_string(),
            profile: Profile {
                bio: Some("Software Engineer".to_string()),
                age: 30,
            },
        });
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_aggregates() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE sales (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                amount REAL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (10.5)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (20.0)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (30.5)", []).unwrap();

        let sum: f64 = conn.query_row("SELECT SUM(amount) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        let avg: f64 = conn.query_row("SELECT AVG(amount) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        let count: i64 = conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(sum, 61.0);
        assert_eq!(avg, 20.333333333333332);
        assert_eq!(count, 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_joins_and_limits() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE authors (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE books (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                title TEXT NOT NULL,
                author_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                FOREIGN KEY(author_id) REFERENCES authors(id)
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES ('J.R.R. Tolkien')", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES ('George R.R. Martin')", []).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('The Hobbit', 1)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('The Silmarillion', 1)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('A Game of Thrones', 2)", []).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
            "SELECT b.title, a.name 
             FROM books b 
             JOIN authors a ON b.author_id = a.id 
             ORDER BY b.title 
             LIMIT 2",
        ).unwrap();

        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, String>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {
            results.push(item.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("A Game of Thrones", "George R.R. Martin"));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("The Hobbit", "J.R.R. Tolkien"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_statement_reuse() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE items (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("INSERT INTO items (name) VALUES (?)").unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["A"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["B"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["C"]).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name FROM items WHERE id = ?").unwrap();
        let row1 = stmt.query_row([1], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).unwrap();
        let row2 = stmt.query_row([2], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).unwrap();
        let row3 = stmt.query_row([3], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(row1, "A");
        assert_eq!(row2, "B");
        assert_eq!(row3, "C");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_file_persistence() {
        let path = "test_db.db";
        // Ensure the file doesn't exist
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);

        {
            let conn = Connection::open(path).unwrap();
            conn.execute(
                "CREATE TABLE test (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, val TEXT)",
                [],
            ).unwrap();
            conn.execute("INSERT INTO test (val) VALUES ('hello')", []).unwrap();
        } // Connection closed

        {
            let conn = Connection::open(path).unwrap();
            let val: String = conn.query_row("SELECT val FROM test", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(val, "hello");
        }

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_error_verification() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO users (name) VALUES ('Alice')", []).unwrap();
        
        // This should fail due to UNIQUE constraint
        let result = conn.execute("INSERT INTO users (name) VALUES ('Alice')", []);
        assert!(result.is_err());
        
        let err = result.unwrap_err();
        // Check if it's a Constraint violation (SQLITE_CONSTRAINT)
        assert_eq!(err.sqlite_error_code(), Some(rusqlite::ErrorCode::ConstraintViolation));
    }
}

fn main() {
    println!("Hello, world!");
}

use rusqlite::{params, Connection, Result};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    struct Profile {
        bio: Option<String>,
        age: i32,
    }

    #[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
    struct User {
        id: i32,
        name: String,
        profile: Profile,
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_open_in_memory_connection() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory();
        assert!(conn.is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_create_table_and_insert() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name  TEXT NOT NULL,
                age   INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Alice", 30],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, age) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
            params!["Bob", 25],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, age FROM users ORDER BY age").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, i32>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("Bob".to_string(), 25));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("Alice".to_string(), 30));

    #[test]

        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE products (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("INSERT INTO products (name) VALUES (?)").unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Laptop"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["Phone"]).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name FROM products").unwrap();
        let names: Vec<String> = stmt.query_map([], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap().map(|r| r.unwrap()).collect();
        assert_eq!(names, vec!["Laptop", "Phone"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        tx.commit().unwrap();

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 900);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_transaction_rollback() {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE accounts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, balance INTEGER)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO accounts (id, balance) VALUES (1, 1000)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let tx = conn.transaction().unwrap();
        tx.execute("UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - 100 WHERE id = 1", []).unwrap();
        // Drop transaction without commit to rollback
        drop(tx);

        let balance: i32 = conn.query_row("SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id = 1", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(balance, 1000);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_multiple_types_and_nullability() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE items (
                id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

            )",

        // Test with a NULL description

        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name, price, description, data FROM items").unwrap();
        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((
                row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
                row.get::<_, f64>(1)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<String>>(2)?,
                row.get::<_, Option<Vec<u8>>>(3)?,

        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {

    #[test]

        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name     TEXT NOT NULL,
                bio      TEXT,
                age      INTEGER NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (name, bio, age) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
            params!["Steven", Some("Software Engineer".to_string()), 30],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT id, name, bio, age FROM users").unwrap();
        let user_iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok(User {
                id: row.get(0)?,
                name: row.get(1)?,
                profile: Profile {
                    bio: row.get(2)?,
                    age: row.get(3)?,
                },
            })
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for user in user_iter {
            results.push(user.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(results[0], User {
            id: 1,
            name: "Steven".to_string(),
            profile: Profile {
                bio: Some("Software Engineer".to_string()),
                age: 30,
            },
        });
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_aggregates() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE sales (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                amount REAL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (10.5)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (20.0)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO sales (amount) VALUES (30.5)", []).unwrap();

        let sum: f64 = conn.query_row("SELECT SUM(amount) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        let avg: f64 = conn.query_row("SELECT AVG(amount) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
        let count: i64 = conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sales", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(sum, 61.0);
        assert_eq!(avg, 20.333333333333332);
        assert_eq!(count, 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_joins_and_limits() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE authors (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE books (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                title TEXT NOT NULL,
                author_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                FOREIGN KEY(author_id) REFERENCES authors(id)
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES ('J.R.R. Tolkien')", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES ('George R.R. Martin')", []).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('The Hobbit', 1)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('The Silmarillion', 1)", []).unwrap();
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO books (title, author_id) VALUES ('A Game of Thrones', 2)", []).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
            "SELECT b.title, a.name 
             FROM books b 
             JOIN authors a ON b.author_id = a.id 
             ORDER BY b.title 
             LIMIT 2",
        ).unwrap();

        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
            Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, String>(1)?))
        }).unwrap();

        let mut results = Vec::new();
        for item in iter {
            results.push(item.unwrap());
        }

        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(results[0], ("A Game of Thrones", "George R.R. Martin"));
        assert_eq!(results[1], ("The Hobbit", "J.R.R. Tolkien"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_statement_reuse() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE items (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("INSERT INTO items (name) VALUES (?)").unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["A"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["B"]).unwrap();
        stmt.execute(params!["C"]).unwrap();

        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT name FROM items WHERE id = ?").unwrap();
        let row1 = stmt.query_row([1], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).unwrap();
        let row2 = stmt.query_row([2], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).unwrap();
        let row3 = stmt.query_row([3], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(row1, "A");
        assert_eq!(row2, "B");
        assert_eq!(row3, "C");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_file_persistence() {
        let path = "test_db.db";
        // Ensure the file doesn't exist
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);

        {
            let conn = Connection::open(path).unwrap();
            conn.execute(
                "CREATE TABLE test (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, val TEXT)",
                [],
            ).unwrap();
            conn.execute("INSERT INTO test (val) VALUES ('hello')", []).unwrap();
        } // Connection closed

        {
            let conn = Connection::open(path).unwrap();
            let val: String = conn.query_row("SELECT val FROM test", [], |row| row.get(0)).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(val, "hello");
        }

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_error_verification() {
        let conn = Connection::open_in_memory().unwrap();
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE users (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
            )",
            [],
        ).unwrap();

        conn.execute("INSERT INTO users (name) VALUES ('Alice')", []).unwrap();
        
        // This should fail due to UNIQUE constraint
        let result = conn.execute("INSERT INTO users (name) VALUES ('Alice')", []);
        assert!(result.is_err());
        
        let err = result.unwrap_err();
        // Check if it's a Constraint violation (SQLITE_CONSTRAINT)
        assert_eq!(err.sqlite_error_code(), Some(rusqlite::ErrorCode::ConstraintViolation));
    }
}

fn main() {
    println!("Hello, world!");
}

I have completed the plan to expand the test suite for the `rusqlite` library. All 12 tests (5 original and 7 new) now pass successfully.

### Summary of New Tests Added:
1.  **`test_multiple_types_and_nullability`**: Verified support for various Rust types (`i32`, `f64`, `String`, `Vec<u8>`) and their mapping to SQLite types, including handling of `NULL` values using `Option<T>`.
2.  **`test_complex_struct`**: Demonstrated mapping a nested Rust struct (`User` containing a `Profile` struct) to a database table.
3.  **`test_aggregates`**: Verified standard SQL aggregate functions (`SUM`, `AVG`, `COUNT`) work correctly with `rusqlite`.
4.  **`test_joins_and_limits`**: Verified complex queries involving `JOIN` clauses, `ORDER BY`, and `LIMIT` clauses.
5.  **`test_statement_reuse`**: Confirmed that prepared statements can be reused for multiple executions with different parameters.
6.  **`test_file_persistence`**: Verified that data is correctly persisted to a physical file on disk and can be retrieved after closing the connection.
7.  **`test_error_verification`**: Ensured that the library correctly identifies and reports SQLite error codes (e.g., `ConstraintViolation`).

The project is now a robust testbed for verifying `rusqlite` core features in an offline environment.

Since I am operating in an environment where I cannot browse the live internet, I used the following strategies to gather information:

### 1. Compiler Feedback (The "Interactive" Manual)
